  Have: forall i_5 : Z. ((0 <= i_5) -> ((i_5 < n) ->
      valid_rd(Malloc_0, shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(graph_0, i_5), 0), n))).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: valid_rw(Malloc_0, a_1, x).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: forall i_5 : Z. ((0 <= i_5) -> ((i_5 < n) ->
      valid_rw(Malloc_0, shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(dist_0, i_5), 0), n))).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: separated(a, x, a_1, x).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_matrix_bounded(Mint_0, graph_0, n).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_diagonal_zero(Mint_0, graph_0, n).
  (* Loop assigns ... *)
  Have: forall a_2 : addr.
      ((forall i_6,i_5 : Z. ((0 <= i_6) -> ((i_6 < n) -> ((0 <= i_5) ->
        ((i_5 < n) ->
        (shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(dist_0, i_6), i_5) != a_2)))))) ->
      (Mint_1[a_2] = Mint_0[a_2])).
  (* Invariant 'i_0' *)
  Have: (0 <= i) /\ (i <= n).
  (* Invariant 'i_1' *)
  Have: forall i_6,i_5 : Z. ((0 <= i_6) -> ((i_6 < i) -> ((0 <= i_5) ->
      ((i_5 < n) ->
      (Mint_1[shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(graph_0, i_6), i_5)]
         = Mint_1[shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(dist_0, i_6), i_5)]))))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n <= i.
  (* Invariant 'i_3' *)
  Have: P_matrix_bounded(Mint_1, dist_0, i).
  (* Invariant 'i_4' *)
  Have: P_diagonal_zero(Mint_1, dist_0, i).
  (* Invariant 'i_5' *)
  Have: P_no_larger_than_graph(Mint_0, Mint_1, graph_0, dist_0, i).
  (* Loop assigns ... *)
  Have: forall a_2 : addr.
      ((forall i_6,i_5 : Z. ((0 <= i_6) -> ((i_6 < i) -> ((0 <= i_5) ->
        ((i_5 < i) ->
        (shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(dist_0, i_6), i_5) != a_2)))))) ->
      (Mint_1[a_2] = Mint_2[a_2])).
}
Prove: exists i_5 : Z. let x_2 = 100 * i_5 in (0 <= i_5) /\ (i_5 < i) /\
    (x_2 <= x_1) /\ (x_1 < (i + x_2)).
